4. "Which sentence has parallel elements: The team practiced passing, shooting, and defense, or The team practiced passing, shooting, and defending?" Answer: The team practiced passing, shooting, and defending. Hint: Compare the form of every item in each list, and choose the sentence where all the items match.
5. "Which sentence has parallel elements: Ana came to learn, practicing, and to compete, or Ana came to learn, to practice, and to compete?" Answer: Ana came to learn, to practice, and to compete. Hint: Compare the form of every item in each list, and choose the sentence where all the items match.
6. "Which sentence has parallel elements: Leo likes apples, oranges, and bananas, or Leo likes apples, oranges, and to eat bananas?" Answer: Leo likes apples, oranges, and bananas. Hint: Compare the form of every item in each list, and choose the sentence where all the items match.
7. "Which sentence has parallel elements: The book is long, detailed, and interest, or The book is long, detailed, and interesting?" Answer: The book is long, detailed, and interesting. Hint: Compare the form of every item in each list, and choose the sentence where all the items match.
8. "Which sentence has parallel elements: Sam walked, talked, and laughed on the trail, or Sam walked, talked, and laughter on the trail?" Answer: Sam walked, talked, and laughed on the trail. Hint: Compare the form of every item in each list, and choose the sentence where all the items match.
9. "Which sentence has parallel elements: Ana will study, review, and practicing tonight, or Ana will study, review, and practice tonight?" Answer: Ana will study, review, and practice tonight. Hint: Compare the form of every item in each list, and choose the sentence where all the items match.
10. "Which sentence has parallel elements: Leo prefers reading to watching, or Leo prefers reading to watch?" Answer: Leo prefers reading to watching. Hint: Compare the form of every item in each list, and choose the sentence where all the items match.
11. "Which sentence has parallel elements: The garden needs water, sunlight, and caring, or The garden needs water, sunlight, and care?" Answer: The garden needs water, sunlight, and care. Hint: Compare the form of every item in each list, and choose the sentence where all the items match.
12. "Which sentence has parallel elements: Sam likes to hike, to swim, and to camp, or Sam likes hiking, to swim, and to camp?" Answer: Sam likes to hike, to swim, and to camp. Hint: Compare the form of every item in each list, and choose the sentence where all the items match.

### Matching Parallel Elements 1

**Teaching card**
Definition: To match parallel elements, revise the item that breaks the pattern so it uses the same form as the other items, such as changing a noun to a gerund or an infinitive to match the rest of the list.
Worked example: Ana likes reading, writing, and to speak becomes Ana likes reading, writing, and speaking. Labels: to speak, nonparallel infinitive; speaking, revised gerund to match reading and writing.
How it works: Find the form used by most items in the list, then change the odd item to that form so the whole series reads smoothly.
Tip box: Leo enjoys swimming, biking, and to run becomes Leo enjoys swimming, biking, and running. Sam is smart, kind, and honesty becomes Sam is smart, kind, and honest.

**Quiz**
1. "Which revision is parallel: Leo enjoys swimming, biking, and running, or Leo enjoys swimming, biking, and to run?" Answer: Leo enjoys swimming, biking, and running. Hint: Find the form most items share, and choose the revision where the odd item has been changed to match it.
2. "Which revision is parallel: Ana likes to read, writing, and to speak, or Ana likes to read, to write, and to speak?" Answer: Ana likes to read, to write, and to speak. Hint: Find the form most items share, and choose the revision where the odd item has been changed to match it.
3. "Which revision is parallel: Sam is brave, loyal, and honest, or Sam is brave, loyal, and honesty?" Answer: Sam is brave, loyal, and honest. Hint: Find the form most items share, and choose the revision where the odd item has been changed to match it.
4. "Which revision is parallel: The class studied maps, charts, and to read graphs, or The class studied maps, charts, and graphs?" Answer: The class studied maps, charts, and graphs. Hint: Find the form most items share, and choose the revision where the odd item has been changed to match it.
5. "Which revision is parallel: Leo will clean, cook, and washing dishes, or Leo will clean, cook, and wash dishes?" Answer: Leo will clean, cook, and wash dishes. Hint: Find the form most items share, and choose the revision where the odd item has been changed to match it.
6. "Which revision is parallel: Ana came to learn, to practice, and to compete, or Ana came to learn, practicing, and to compete?" Answer: Ana came to learn, to practice, and to compete. Hint: Find the form most items share, and choose the revision where the odd item has been changed to match it.
7. "Which revision is parallel: The team practiced passing, shooting, and defense, or The team practiced passing, shooting, and defending?" Answer: The team practiced passing, shooting, and defending. Hint: Find the form most items share, and choose the revision where the odd item has been changed to match it.
8. "Which revision is parallel: Sam likes apples, oranges, and bananas, or Sam likes apples, oranges, and to eat bananas?" Answer: Sam likes apples, oranges, and bananas. Hint: Find the form most items share, and choose the revision where the odd item has been changed to match it.
9. "Which revision is parallel: Leo prefers reading to watch, or Leo prefers reading to watching?" Answer: Leo prefers reading to watching. Hint: Find the form most items share, and choose the revision where the odd item has been changed to match it.
10. "Which revision is parallel: Ana walked, talked, and laughed, or Ana walked, talked, and laughter?" Answer: Ana walked, talked, and laughed. Hint: Find the form most items share, and choose the revision where the odd item has been changed to match it.
11. "Which revision is parallel: The book is long, detailed, and interest, or The book is long, detailed, and interesting?" Answer: The book is long, detailed, and interesting. Hint: Find the form most items share, and choose the revision where the odd item has been changed to match it.
12. "Which revision is parallel: Sam likes hiking, to swim, and to camp, or Sam likes to hike, to swim, and to camp?" Answer: Sam likes to hike, to swim, and to camp. Hint: Find the form most items share, and choose the revision where the odd item has been changed to match it.

## Pathway: Possessives (1 topic)

### Forming Singular Possessives

**Teaching card**
Definition: A singular possessive shows that one person, animal, or thing owns something, and it is formed by adding an apostrophe and s to the singular noun, even when the noun already ends in s.
Worked example: The dog's leash hung by the door, and the class's project won first place. Labels: dog's, singular possessive of dog; class's, singular possessive of class.
How it works: Find the single owner, add an apostrophe and s to that noun, and place the owned thing right after it.
Tip box: Ana's notebook is on the desk. The bus's brakes were checked before the trip.

**Quiz**
1. "Write the correct singular possessive form of dog in this sentence: The ___ leash hung by the door." Answer: dog's. Hint: Add an apostrophe and s to a singular noun to show ownership.
2. "Write the correct singular possessive form of Ana in this sentence: ___ notebook is on the desk." Answer: Ana's. Hint: Add an apostrophe and s to a singular noun to show ownership.
3. "Write the correct singular possessive form of class in this sentence: The ___ project won first place." Answer: class's. Hint: Add an apostrophe and s even when the singular noun ends in s.
4. "Write the correct singular possessive form of teacher in this sentence: The ___ desk is near the window." Answer: teacher's. Hint: Add an apostrophe and s to a singular noun to show ownership.
5. "Write the correct singular possessive form of Sam in this sentence: ___ bike is parked by the gym." Answer: Sam's. Hint: Add an apostrophe and s to a singular noun to show ownership.
6. "Write the correct singular possessive form of bus in this sentence: The ___ brakes were checked before the trip." Answer: bus's. Hint: Add an apostrophe and s even when the singular noun ends in s.
7. "Write the correct singular possessive form of Leo in this sentence: ___ jacket is hanging in the hall." Answer: Leo's. Hint: Add an apostrophe and s to a singular noun to show ownership.
8. "Write the correct singular possessive form of school in this sentence: The ___ library opens at eight." Answer: school's. Hint: Add an apostrophe and s to a singular noun to show ownership.
9. "Write the correct singular possessive form of James in this sentence: ___ report is due on Friday." Answer: James's. Hint: Add an apostrophe and s even when the singular noun ends in s.
10. "Write the correct singular possessive form of cat in this sentence: The ___ tail curled around the chair leg." Answer: cat's. Hint: Add an apostrophe and s to a singular noun to show ownership.
11. "Write the correct singular possessive form of girl in this sentence: The ___ science project used plants." Answer: girl's. Hint: Add an apostrophe and s to a singular noun to show ownership.
12. "Write the correct singular possessive form of boss in this sentence: The ___ office is on the second floor." Answer: boss's. Hint: Add an apostrophe and s even when the singular noun ends in s.
